# **Orders API**

The order APIs let you place orders of various types, modify or cancel pending orders, and retrieve daily order history. With OMS (Order Management System), you can manage your trades efficiently, execute orders in real time, track their status, and maintain full control over your portfolio.

These endpoints let you place, modify, cancel, and retrieve orders efficiently.

```
BASE URL : https://{baseurl}/api_interactive/api/v1
```

| **Type** | **Endpoint**                                             | **Description**                                                 |
| -------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
| POST     | [orders/placeOrder](#place-order)                        | Place an order of a particular variety.                         |
| PUT      | [orders/modifyOrder](#modify-order)                      | Modify an open or pending order.                                |
| DELETE   | [orders/cancelOrder](#cancel-order)                      | Cancel an open or pending order.                                |
| GET      | [orders](#fetching-orders)                               | Retrieve the list of all orders (open and executed) for the day.|
| GET      | [orders/openOrders](#fetch-open-orders)                  | Retrieve all open or pending orders.                            |
| GET      | [orders/{blitzOrderId}](#get-order-by-id)                | Retrieve the full history (lifecycle) of a specific order.      |

!!! note "**Notes**"
    - placeOrder → Endpoint to create a new order.
    - modifyOrder → Endpoint to update an existing order.
    - cancelOrder → Endpoint to delete/cancel an order.

---

## What is `blitzOrderId`, and why is it required?

The `blitzOrderId` is a **unique, system-assigned order identifier** (a large `integer`, e.g. `222115050150000006`) that the platform generates when your order is first placed.

Key points:

- It is returned in the **response of every place-order call** — use it to track, modify, cancel, and retrieve the lifecycle of that specific order.
- It identifies **your order across the entire OMS**, unlike the `CorrelationOrderId` (a client-supplied tag) or the `ExchangeOrderId` (the counterpart ID assigned by the exchange once the order is sent out).
- It is the value you pass to `orders/{blitzOrderId}`, `trades/{blitzOrderId}`, and `cancelOrder` so the API knows exactly which order you mean.
- One order has **one** `blitzOrderId` for life, even if it is modified, partially filled, or cancelled — every subsequent status change and fill event stays linked to that same ID.

**Typical flow:**

1. Place an order → the response returns `blitzOrderId`.
2. Track it via `GET /orders/{blitzOrderId}` to see every status-change event.
3. Check fills via `GET /trades/{blitzOrderId}` to see every executed quantity and price.
4. Modify or cancel it using the same `blitzOrderId`.

> In the endpoint table above, `orders/{blitzOrderId}` and `trades/{blitzOrderId}` expect this **numeric** system ID in the path — not the `CorrelationOrderId` and not the `ExchangeOrderId`.

---

# Place an Order {#place-order}

This API enables order placement across all supported instruments. A successful request returns a unique order_id that can be used for order tracking, modification, and cancellation. Additionally, a custom tag can be assigned to categorize and easily retrieve related orders using the Order History API.

!!! note "**Notes**"
    Successful API submission does not guarantee execution at the exchange. The response only confirms that the order has been registered with the system.

## Examples

=== "By Instrument ID"

    ```bash
    curl -X 'POST' \
      'https://{baseurl}/api_interactive/api/v1/orders/placeOrder' \
      -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      -H 'Accept: application/json' \
      -H 'Authorization: Bearer {access_token}' \
      -d '{
      "CorrelationOrderId": "corr_456",
      "Quantity": 500,
      "Product": "MIS",
      "TIF": "GTD",
      "Price": 10.1,
      "OrderType": "Market",
      "ExchangeSegment": "NSECM",
      "InstrumentId": 110010000002885,
      "OrderSide": "Buy",
      "DisclosedQuantity": 0,
      "StopPrice": 0,
      "ClientId": "Algo123",
      "TiF_GTD_Date": "2025-10-10"
    }'
    ```

=== "By Symbol"

=== "By Symbol (Weekly Option)"

    ```bash
    curl -X 'POST' \
      'https://{baseurl}/api_interactive/api/v1/orders/placeOrder' \
      -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      -H 'Accept: application/json' \

      "CorrelationOrderId": "corr_457",

      "Product": "MIS",
      "TIF": "GFD",
      "Price": 85.0,
      "OrderType": "Limit",
      "ExchangeSegment": "NSEFO",
      "Symbol": "NSEFO|NIFTY25MAR261030CE",
      "OrderSide": "Buy",
      "DisclosedQuantity": 0,
      "StopPrice": 0,
      "ClientId": "Algo123",
      "TiF_GTD_Date": "2025-10-10"
    }'
    ```

> The `TiF_GTD_Date` field is **required** for every order. A weekly-expiry option name follows the pattern `[ExchangeSegment]|[Symbol][DDMMMYY][Strike][OptionType]` — e.g. `NSEFO|NIFTY25MAR261030CE` is the NIFTY **1030 CE weekly** option expiring **25 Mar 2026**. The expiry date identifies whether the contract is a weekly or monthly series.

=== "By ExchangeInstrumentId"

    ```bash
    curl -X 'POST' \
      'https://{baseurl}/api_interactive/api/v1/orders/placeOrder' \
      -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      -H 'Accept: application/json' \
      -H 'Authorization: Bearer {access_token}' \
      -d '{

      "Quantity": 500,

      "TiF_GTD_Date": "2025-10-10"
    }'
    ```

### Response Body

```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "message": "request processed successfully",
  "data": {
    "blitzOrderId": 218100636600000022,
    "correlationOrderId": "order_1787046999088"
  }
}
```

### Field Descriptions

| Field              | Type    | Description                                                                                     |
| ------------------ | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CorrelationOrderId` | string  | A client-generated unique identifier used to track and correlate orders across your system      |
| `Quantity`           | integer | Quantity of the order to be placed                                                              |
| `Product`            | string  | They define whether an order is intended for intraday trading, delivery-based investing, margin trading, or other trading objectives, influencing factors such as position holding period, margin requirements, and settlement behavior. |
| `TIF`                | string  | Time-in-force for the order (`IOC`, `GTD`)                                                      |
| `Price`              | number  | Price at which the order should be executed                                                     |
| `OrderType`          | string  | Type of order (`Market`, `Limit`, `Stop`, `StopLimit`) |
| `ExchangeSegment`     | string  | **Mandatory.** Exchange segment of the instrument (`NSECM`, `NSEFO`, `NSECD`, `BSECM`, `BSEFO`, `BSECD`, `MCXFO`). |
| `Symbol`       | string  | Optional. Trading symbol of the instrument (e.g. `NSEFO\|RELIANCE24FEB261030CE`).               |
| `InstrumentId`        | integer | Optional. Internal Blitz instrument ID (e.g. `110010000002885`).                                |
| `ExchangeInstrumentId`| integer | Optional. Exchange instrument ID / universal ID (e.g. `2885` or the 16-digit universal ID).      |
| `OrderSide`          | string  | Side of the order (`Buy`, `Sell`)                                                               |
| `DisclosedQuantity`  | integer | Quantity disclosed for iceberg orders                                                           |
| `StopPrice`          | number  | Trigger price for Stop-Loss (`Stop`) or Stop-Limit (`StopLimit`) orders.                        |
| `ClientId`           | string  | **Required.** ID of the client (exchange client) placing the order. It is validated against the current session and its type (Individual / Proprietary / Dealer). |
| `TiF_GTD_Date`       | string  | **Required.** Expiry date in `YYYY-MM-DD` format. Required for **every** order — even for `GFD`/`GTC` orders the field must be present (e.g. today's date). If the GTD order is not executed by this date, it is automatically cancelled. |

> **Instrument identification — exactly one identifier is required**
> `ExchangeSegment` is always **mandatory**. Supply **exactly one** of the following along with it:
>
> | Combination                       | Example                                       |
> | --------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- |
> | `ExchangeSegment` + `Symbol`       | `NSECM` + `NSEFO\|RELIANCE24FEB261030CE` |
> | `ExchangeSegment` + `InstrumentId`        | `NSEFO` + `110010000002885`              |
> | `ExchangeSegment` + `ExchangeInstrumentId`| `NSEFO` + `2885` (or the 16-digit universal ID) |
>
> - If more than one identifier is provided, `InstrumentId` takes precedence over `ExchangeInstrumentId`, which takes precedence over `Symbol`.
> - The order must be placed using **either** a valid `InstrumentId` **or** a valid `ExchangeInstrumentId` **or** a valid `Symbol`. If the given identifier is not found, the API returns an error containing the reason (e.g. instrument not found for the provided identifier).

> **Authentication & ClientId**
> - `UserId` is **not required** in the request payload — it is stored inherently in the API key session.
> - `ClientId` is **required** for every order. The backend validates that the provided `ClientId` is valid for the session (Individual / Proprietary / Dealer) before placing the order.

> **Case sensitivity**
> All string values (`ExchangeSegment`, `Symbol`, `Product`, `OrderType`, `OrderSide`, `TIF`) are **case-sensitive** and must match the enum names exactly as documented (e.g. `Buy`, `NSECM`, `Limit`).

!!! note "**Notes**"

    **Product types**

    | Product   | Description                                                                                                                      |
    | --------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
    | `MIS`     | **Intraday.** Positions are squared off automatically at the end of the trading day. Attracts lower margin.                       |
    | `NRML`    | **Normal / carry forward.** Positions can be carried to the next trading day.                                                     |
    | `CNC`     | **Cash & Carry / Delivery.** Positions are carried forward and settled via delivery. Requires full margin.                        |
    | `MTF`     | **Margin Trading Facility.** Trade with borrowed funds against a margin.                                                          |

    **Order types**

    | Order type             | Description                                                                                      |
    | ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
    | `Market`               | Execute immediately at the best available market price. Speed is guaranteed, the fill price is not. |
    | `Limit`                | Execute only at the specified price or better. Price is guaranteed, execution is not until the price is reached. |
    | `Stop`                 | Stop-loss order. Triggers a market execution once the market reaches the stop price.             |
    | `StopLimit`            | Stop-limit order. Triggers a limit order once the market reaches the stop price.                 |
  


    **Time in Force (TIF)**

    | TIF   | Description                                                                                                                                 |
    | ----- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
    | `GFD` | Good For Day. Valid for the trading day. Any unfilled quantity is canceled automatically at the end of the day.                             |
    | `GTC` | Good Till Cancel. The order stays live until it fills or you cancel it explicitly.                                                          |
    | `IOC` | Immediate or Cancel. Fill as much as possible right now, cancel the remainder instantly. No order stays resting on the book.                |
    | `FOK` | Fill Or Kill. Fill the entire order immediately, otherwise the whole order is canceled.                                                     |
    | `GTD` | Good Till Date. The order stays live until a specific expiry date/time that you specify.                                                    |

    > The `Product`, `OrderType` and `TIF` values above are **case-sensitive** and must match the enum names exactly as documented (e.g. `Limit`, not `LIMIT`).

### Response Codes

| HTTP Code | Description                                                                        |
| --------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **200**   | **Order successfully placed.** The order has been accepted and registered in the OMS system. |
| **400**   | **Bad Request.** One or more parameters are invalid or missing in the request body. The response includes the exact reason (e.g. `ExchangeSegment` is mandatory; missing `ClientId`; `OrderQuantity`, `Price`, `OrderType` invalid for the product type). |
| **401**   | **Unauthorized.** The request is missing a valid JWT token or the token has expired. |
| **404**   | **Not Found.** The response includes the reason — e.g. **instrument not found** for the provided `InstrumentId`, `ExchangeInstrumentId` or `Symbol`; or invalid `ClientId` for the session. |
| **422**   | **Unprocessable Entity.** The `ClientId` is not valid for the current session type (Individual / Proprietary / Dealer). |
| **429**   | **Too Many Requests.** Throttling: the platform applies a rate limit to protect the exchange and gateway. When you hit the limit, throttle back and honor the `Retry-After` header on the response before sending more orders. |
| **500**   | **Internal Server Error.** A system or exchange-level error occurred while processing the order. |

!!! note "**Retry safety & token refresh**"
    - **Idempotent retry.** Set your own `CorrelationOrderId` on every place request. If a request times out or fails with a network error (it is unclear whether the exchange accepted it), **do not blindly re-place** — first call [`GET /orders`](#fetching-orders) or [`GET /orders/{blitzOrderId}`](#get-order-by-blitzorderid) to check whether the order was actually created, then retry once with the same `CorrelationOrderId` so retries map to the same logical request.
    - **Token expiry.** An expired JWT fails with `401 Unauthorized`. Re-authenticate through the **App Login** endpoint to get a fresh `accessToken`, then retry with the new token. Never cache or reuse expired tokens.

!!! tip "**Tip for Placing Orders**"
    - Always confirm the **order status** after placement using the returned `orderId` or `exchangeOrderId`.
    - Ensure **sufficient margin/funds** are available before placing the order to prevent rejections.
    - Place orders **only during active market hours** for faster acknowledgment and execution.

---

# Modify Order {#modify-order}

The **Modify Order** endpoint allows you to update an existing order's parameters such as **quantity**, **price**, **stop price**, or **validity**.

Use this API to adjust open or pending orders before they are executed at the exchange.

!!! note "**Notes**"
    - Only orders in *open* or *pending* states can be modified.
    - Once an order is executed or cancelled, modification requests will be rejected.
    - After modification, query the [orders](#fetching-orders) to confirm that the changes were successfully applied.

---

### Field Descriptions

| Field                 | Type    | Description                                                               |
| --------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `BlitzOrderId`          | integer | Order ID assigned by the system                                           |
| `ModifiedOrderQuantity` | integer | Updated quantity of the order                                             |
| `Price`                 | number  | Updated price at which the order should be executed                       |
| `OrderType`             | string  | Type of order (`Market`, `Limit`, `Stop`, `StopLimit`) |
| `ExchangeSegment`       | string  | **Mandatory.** Exchange segment of the instrument (`NSECM`, `NSEFO`).     |
| `Symbol`         | string  | Optional. Trading symbol of the instrument.                               |
| `InstrumentId`          | integer | Optional. Internal Blitz instrument ID.                                   |
| `ExchangeInstrumentId`  | integer | Optional. Exchange instrument ID / universal ID.                          |
| `DisclosedQuantity`     | integer | Updated disclosed quantity for the order                                  |
| `StopPrice`             | number  | Updated stop price for stop or stop-limit orders                          |
| `TIF`                   | string  | Time-in-force for the order (`GFD`, `GTC`, `IOC`, `FOK`, `GTD`)    |
| `TiF_GTD_Date`          | string  | Expiry date for GTD orders in `YYYY-MM-DD` format                         |

> `ExchangeSegment` is **mandatory**. Supply **exactly one** of `Symbol`, `InstrumentId` or `ExchangeInstrumentId` along with it, as described in the [Place Order](#place-order) field table above.

## Examples

=== "By Instrument ID"

    ```bash
    curl -X 'PUT' \
      'https://{baseurl}/api_interactive/api/v1/orders/modifyOrder' \
      -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      -H 'Accept: application/json' \
      -H 'Authorization: Bearer {access_token}' \
      -d '{
      "ModifiedOrderQuantity": 1000,
      "Price": 10.8,
      "BlitzOrderId": 227134718870000009,
      "OrderType": "Limit",
      "ExchangeSegment": "NSECM",
      "InstrumentId": 110010000002885,
      "DisclosedQuantity": 0,
      "StopPrice": 0,
      "TIF": "GTD",
      "TiF_GTD_Date": "2025-10-10"
    }'
    ```

=== "By Symbol"

    ```bash
    curl -X 'PUT' \
      'https://{baseurl}/api_interactive/api/v1/orders/modifyOrder' \
      -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      -H 'Accept: application/json' \
      -H 'Authorization: Bearer {access_token}' \
      -d '{
      "ModifiedOrderQuantity": 1000,
      "Price": 10.8,
      "BlitzOrderId": 227134718870000009,
      "OrderType": "Limit",
      "Symbol": "NSECM|IDEA",
      "DisclosedQuantity": 0,
      "StopPrice": 0,
      "TIF": "GTD",
      "TiF_GTD_Date": "2025-10-10"
    }'
    ```

### Response Body

```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "message": "request processed successfully",
  "data": "successfully send modify order request to blitz server"
}
```

### Response Codes

| HTTP Code | Description                                                                                  |
| --------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **200**   | **Order modified successfully.** The order update has been accepted by the Blitz system.     |
| **400**   | **Bad Request.** One or more parameters are invalid or missing.                              |
| **401**   | **Unauthorized.** Missing or invalid JWT token.                                              |
| **403**   | **Forbidden.** The order cannot be modified (already executed or cancelled).                 |
| **500**   | **Internal Server Error.** A system or exchange-level error occurred while processing the modification. |

!!! tip "**Tip for Modifying Orders**"
    - Always verify the **current order status** before attempting to modify it. Only open or pending orders can be changed.
    - Modify orders **during market hours** to ensure updates are accepted and reflected quickly.
    - Avoid frequent or large modifications in short intervals to prevent exchange-level throttling or rejection.
    - Confirm the **modified parameters** (price, quantity, stop price) meet exchange and instrument-specific limits.

---

# Cancel Order {#cancel-order}

The **Cancel Order** endpoint allows you to cancel an existing open or pending order using its unique instrument identifier (`ExchangeSegment` + `ExchangeInstrumentId`, or `symbol`) and `blitzOrderId`.

!!! note "**Notes**"
    - Only orders that are in *open* or *pending* state can be cancelled.
    - Orders that are already executed or expired cannot be cancelled.

### Parameters

| Name                | Type    | Description                                                                 |
| ------------------- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ExchangeSegment`   | string  | **Mandatory.** Exchange segment of the instrument (`NSECM`, `NSEFO`).       |
| `Symbol`     | string  | Optional. Trading symbol of the instrument.                                 |
| `InstrumentId`      | integer | Optional. Internal Blitz instrument ID.                                     |
| `ExchangeInstrumentId`| integer | Optional. Exchange instrument ID / universal ID (e.g. `2885`). **Recommended.** |
| `blitzOrderId`      | integer | System-assigned ID of the order to cancel.                                  |

> `ExchangeSegment` is **mandatory** and **exactly one** of `Symbol`, `InstrumentId` or `ExchangeInstrumentId` must be supplied.

!!! warning "**Verified behavior — use `ExchangeInstrumentId`, not the internal Blitz ID**"
    Passing the internal Blitz ID (`110010000002885`) as `instrumentId` fails with `400 {"error":"invalid instrument"}`. Use the **exchange-level** `ExchangeInstrumentId` (e.g. `2885` for RELIANCE on NSECM) together with `ExchangeSegment`.

## Examples

=== "By ExchangeInstrumentId"

    ```bash
    curl -X 'DELETE' \
      'https://{baseurl}/api_interactive/api/v1/orders/cancelOrder?ExchangeSegment=NSECM&ExchangeInstrumentId=2885&blitzOrderId=227134718870000009' \
      -H 'Accept: application/json' \
      -H 'Authorization: Bearer {access_token}'
    ```

=== "By Symbol"

    ```bash
    curl -X 'DELETE' \
      'https://{baseurl}/api_interactive/api/v1/orders/cancelOrder?ExchangeSegment=NSECM&symbol=NSECM%7CIDEA&blitzOrderId=227134718870000010' \
      -H 'Accept: application/json' \
      -H 'Authorization: Bearer {access_token}'
    ```

### Response

```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "message": "request processed successfully",
  "data": "successfully send cancel order request to blitz server"
}
```

### Response Codes

| HTTP Code | Description                                                                                  |
| --------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **200**   | **Order cancelled successfully.** The order has been removed from the OMS system.            |
| **400**   | **Bad Request.** Invalid or missing parameters.                                              |
| **401**   | **Unauthorized.** Missing or invalid JWT token.                                              |
| **403**   | **Forbidden.** The order cannot be cancelled (already executed or expired).                  |
| **500**   | **Internal Server Error.** A system or exchange-level error occurred while processing the cancellation. |

!!! tip "**Tip for Cancelling Orders**"
    - Confirm that the order is in an **open or pending** state before attempting cancellation. Executed or expired orders cannot be cancelled.
    - Use the correct combination of **ExchangeSegment + ExchangeInstrumentId (or symbol)** and **blitzOrderId** to ensure the intended order is cancelled.
    - Always verify the **cancellation status** using the order status or history endpoint after sending the request.
    - Avoid sending multiple cancellation requests for the same order to prevent duplicate or conflicting actions.

---

# Fetching Orders {#fetching-orders}

Retrieve a complete list of all orders placed by a user for the current trading day. This endpoint does not just list each order once, but returns the **complete lifecycle and history** for all the orders.

It includes every state change an order goes through, such as:
- **New / PendingNew orders:** When orders are first placed or waiting for certain conditions.
- **Filled orders:** When orders have been fully or partially executed.
- **Cancelled orders:** When orders have been cancelled either by the user or the system.

This endpoint provides a **centralized view of your daily trading activity**, giving you a complete log of all order transitions and making it easier to track your trades.

!!! note "Lifecycle log, not a summary"
    `GET /orders` returns **every status-change event** for each order, not one row per order. A single `BlitzOrderId` can appear multiple times (e.g., `PendingNew` → `New` → `Replaced` → `PendingCancel` → `Cancelled`). Use `GET /orders/{blitzOrderId}` to see just one order's complete lifecycle.

```bash
curl -X 'GET' \
  'https://{baseurl}/api_interactive/api/v1/orders' \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H 'Authorization: Bearer {access_token}'
```

### Order Object Fields

| Field                        | Type    | Description                                                                 |
| ---------------------------- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Id`                         | integer | Internal unique identifier for the order event.                             |
| `CorrelationOrderId`         | string  | A client-provided ID to track the order.                                    |
| `EntityId`                  | string  | ID of the entity associated with the order.                                 |
| `StrategyId`                | string  | ID of the strategy used to place the order.                                 |
| `StrategyInstanceId`       | string  | ID of the specific strategy instance.                                       |
| `StrategyInstanceName`     | string  | Name of the strategy instance (e.g., `NSECM|IDEA`).                         |
| `StrategyName`              | string  | Name of the strategy (e.g., `Manual Trading`).                              |
| `IVObjectName`             | string  | Name of the IV object.                                                      |
| `InstrumentId`              | integer | Internal ID of the traded instrument.                                       |
| `ExchangeSegment`           | string  | The exchange segment (e.g., `NSECM`, `NSEFO`).                              |
| `ExchangeInstrumentId`     | integer | Exchange-provided instrument ID.                                            |
| `InstrumentName`            | string  | Name or symbol of the instrument (e.g., `IDEA`).                            |
| `InstrumentType`            | string  | Type of the instrument (`"Equity"`, `"Futures"`, `"Options"`).             |
| `BlitzOrderId`             | integer | System-assigned unique ID for the order.                                    |
| `ExchangeOrderId`          | string  | Order ID assigned by the exchange.                                          |
| `ExecutionId`               | string  | Execution or trade ID assigned by the exchange.                             |
| `Account`                    | string  | Account associated with the order.                                          |
| `ClientId`                  | string  | ID of the client placing the order.                                         |
| `OrderType`                 | string  | Type of order (e.g., `Market`, `Limit`).                                    |
| `OrderSide`                 | string  | Side of the order (`Buy` or `Sell`).                                        |
| `OrderStatus`                     | string  | Current status of the order (e.g., `PendingNew`, `New`, `Filled`, `Cancelled`). |
| `OrderQuantity`             | integer | Total requested quantity.                                                    |
| `OrderPrice`                | number  | The limit price of the order (0 for Market).                                |
| `OrderStopPrice`           | number  | The stop price for stop-loss orders.                                        |
| `OrderTriggerPrice`        | number  | The trigger price for the order.                                            |
| `LastTradedQuantity`       | integer | Quantity traded in the most recent execution.                               |
| `LastTradedPrice`          | number  | Price at which the most recent execution occurred.                          |
| `CumulativeQuantity`        | integer | Total quantity that has been filled so far.                                 |
| `LeavesQuantity`            | integer | Remaining quantity to be filled.                                            |
| `TIF`                        | string  | Time In Force (e.g., `GFD`, `GTC`, `IOC`, `FOK`, `GTD`).           |
| `OrderExpiryDate`          | integer | Expiry date of the order in epoch milliseconds.                             |
| `OrderDisclosedQuantity`   | integer | Quantity disclosed to the market.                                           |
| `MinimumQuantity`           | integer | Minimum quantity to be executed.                                            |
| `OrderGeneratedDateTime`  | integer | Timestamp when the order was generated.                                     |
| `LastRequestDateTime`     | integer | Timestamp of the last request related to this order.                        |
| `ExchangeTransactTime`     | integer | Timestamp of the transaction at the exchange.                               |
| `OrderModificationCount`   | integer | Number of times the order has been modified.                                |
| `OrderTradeCount`          | integer | Number of trades executed for this order.                                   |
| `AverageTradedPrice`       | number  | Average price of all executed trades for this order.                        |
| `AverageTradedValue`       | number  | Total traded value of the order.                                            |
| `IsFictiveOrder`           | boolean | Indicates if the order is fictive or simulated.                             |
| `RejectType`                | string  | Type of rejection, if any (e.g., `None`).                                   |
| `RejectTypeReason`         | string  | Reason for the rejection, if applicable.                                    |
| `OrderTag`                  | string  | Tag applied to the order (e.g., `NormalOrder`).                             |
| `CTCLId`                    | string  | CTCL ID used for the order.                                                 |
| `AlgoId`                    | string  | ID of the algorithm used.                                                   |
| `AlgoCategoryId`           | string  | Category ID of the algorithm.                                               |
| `ClearingFirmId`           | string  | ID of the clearing firm.                                                    |
| `PANId`                     | string  | PAN associated with the account.                                            |
| `IsOrderCompleted`         | boolean | Boolean indicating if the order lifecycle is complete.                      |
| `UserText`                  | string  | Custom user text or remarks.                                                |
| `ExecutionType`             | string  | Type of execution event (e.g., `None`, `New`, `Fill`, `Canceled`).          |
| `StrategyTag`               | string  | Tag associated with the strategy.                                           |
| `SequenceNumber`            | integer | Sequence number of the order event in its lifecycle.                        |

# Fetch Open Orders {#fetch-open-orders}

Retrieve all currently open or pending orders.

```bash
curl -X 'GET' \
  'https://{baseurl}/api_interactive/api/v1/orders/openOrders' \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H 'Authorization: Bearer {access_token}'
```

### Order Object Fields

Each entry uses the exact same [Order Object Fields](#order-object-fields) described under [Fetching Orders](#fetching-orders).

### Response Structure

The response is an array of order objects in the same shape as [Fetching Orders](#fetching-orders), filtered to orders currently in an open or pending state (`PendingNew`, `New`, partially filled).

!!! note "Empty response"
    When no order is currently open for the client, the endpoint returns an empty array `[]`. Newly placed orders appear once they reach an open or pending state.

# Get Order By ID {#get-order-by-id}

Retrieve the full history (lifecycle) of a specific order using its `blitzOrderId`.

```bash
curl -X 'GET' \
  'https://{baseurl}/api_interactive/api/v1/orders/225121947920000028' \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -H 'Accept: application/json' \
  -H 'Authorization: Bearer {access_token}'
```

### Response Structure

Returns an **array** containing the order history entries for the requested `blitzOrderId` — one entry per `OrderStatus` change, in ascending order of `Id` (oldest first). The object shape is identical to the [Fetching Orders](#fetching-orders) response above.

`GET /orders/{blitzOrderId}` returns the **full lifecycle** of an order — all status events, from initial placement (`PendingNew`) through to the terminal state. The example below shows the complete lifecycle of a single order that was placed, modified, and cancelled:
